## Document 012: 115年9月海關進出口貿易初步統計 (2026-10-08)

Extraction status: ok

115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
1. 第 3 季出口較上年同季增 755.9 億美元(+44.6%)，以資通與視聽產
品增 367.6 億美元(+55.0%)、電子零組件增 308.4 億美元(+51.2%)
增加較多。 
2. 累計 1 至 9 月出口較上年同期增 2,089.8 億美元(+46.2%)，其中資
通與視聽產品增 1,222.2 億美元(+72.4%)、電子零組件增 726.6 億
美元(+45.7%)。

等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月對東協出口創新高，對美國、陸港為單月次高 
1. 對美國出口 274.6 億美元，較上年同月增 141.5 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 124.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)、 電子零組件增10.1
億美元(+1.3 倍)、機械增 2.2 億美元(+42.3%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 206.2 億美元，較上年同月增 47.8 億美元
(+30.2%)，其中電子零組件增 34.9 億美元(+30.5%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.7 億美元(+39.0%)、機械增 1.9 億美元(+43.7%)。 
3. 對東協出口 188.8 億美元，較上年同月增 91.2 億美元(+93.4%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 49.7 億美元(+2.2 倍)、電子零組件增 32.0
億美元(+63.3%)、礦產品增 4.8 億美元(+1.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 45.3 億美元，較上年同月增 7.7 億美元(+20.6%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 5.7 億美元(+34.1%)、電子零組件增 2.2 億美
元(+56.6%)，基本金屬及其製品減 0.8 億美元(-20.5%)。 
5. 對日本出口 30.7 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+13.4%)，其
中電子零組件增 1.5 億美元(+11.0%)、基本金屬及其製品增 0.6 億
美元(+32.5%)、機械增 0.5 億美元(+29.1%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月對美國出口 2,077.8 億美元，較上年同期增 773.1 億美
元(+59.3%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,600.9 億美元，增 355.3 億美
元(+28.5%)， 對東協出口1,339.6 億美元 ， 增463.6 億美元(+52.9%)，
對歐洲出口 421.8 億美元 ， 增138.4 億美元(+48.8%)， 對日本出口277.7
億美元，增 58.3 億美元(+26.6%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月自中國大陸與香港進口 135.0 億美元 ， 較上年同月增50.1 億美元
(+59.1%)，自南韓進口 116.3 億美元，增52.6 億美元(+82.5%)，自東
協進口 106.0 億美元，增 52.6 億美元(+98.4%)，自日本進口 64.3 億
美元，增 17.7 億美元(+38.0%)，自歐洲進口 50.2 億美元，增 3.5 億
美元(+7.4%)，自美國進口 48.1 億美元，增 6.5 億美元(+15.5%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增77.6%、
自南韓增 65.8%、自中國大陸與香港增 42.8%、自日本增 25.7%、自
美國增 21.4%、自歐洲增 10.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）主要國家/地區今年 1-8 月外貿景氣普遍走升，以南韓(1-9 月)出口年
增 56.8%最為強勁，香港、新加坡亦分別年增 49.0%、39.8%；中國
大陸、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-7 月)、日本各增
9.7%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-8 月以香港進口年增 50.5%較
突出，新加坡、中國大陸分別年增 37.3%、27.0%，南韓(1-9 月)、德
國(1-7 月)分別增 19.7%、10.0%，日本、美國各增 7.8%、4.2%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

日本：7月以降の設備投資需要はAI関連産業を中心に旺盛
3
 7～9月期の機械受注は増勢回復へ。AI関連業種からの受注がけん引
― ①旺盛なAI需要のもと、関連業種では設備不足感が強まっていたこと、②輸出増が持続するもとで、好業績が期待され
ること、③中東情勢の不確実性が後退したことなどが背景
 先行き設備投資は緩やかな増勢を辿る見込み
― 半導体製造装置をはじめ、AI・データセンター関連品目を中心に受注残が増加しており、目先こうした傾向が続く見通し
― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

世界：AI関連投資の拡大が鉱物需要を押し上げる一方、鈍い資源開発投資の回復ペース
4
 AI関連需要の拡大が銅やレアアースの需給環境に影響
― データセンター建設や電力インフラ整備に伴い、銅やレアアースの需要が拡大。ガリウム等の戦略鉱物への需要も増加
 資源開発投資の回復が遅れるなか、中長期的な供給不足への懸念強まる
―IEAは、2035年に銅が供給量対比で約26％の供給不足に陥ると予測。レアアースは供給不足を回避する見込みながら、

採掘・精錬において中国が支配的地位を占めており、供給元の集中がリスク

### Page 8

世界：ヘッジファンドのレバレッジ拡大が市場ショックを増幅、銀行部門への波及にも警戒
 高まるヘッジファンドの存在感と市場への影響力
― ヘッジファンドの総資産は2013年の約4兆ドルから2026年初には約13兆ドルに拡大。平時には市場の流動性や効率性
を支える一方、ストレス時にはレバレッジ解消や取引集中が価格変動を増幅させる懸念
 ヘッジファンドの損失がプライムブローカーを通じて銀行部門に波及するリスク
―IMFはヘッジファンドの運用成績悪化がプライムブローカーの収益にも悪影響を及ぼすと分析。銀行自身の健全性に加
え、ノンバンクとの相互連関を通じた金融システム全体への波及にも警鐘

11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に

― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト

 ASEAN5では石油の国家備蓄制度や設備が未整備。石油の安定供給に向けた対応の強化が急務

―IEAは東南アジアの電力需要が2030年まで年平均5.4％増加すると予測。産業や冷房、データセンター需要が押し上げ
― 再エネ含む多様な電源による供給拡大や、AEBFでも議論された国際送配電網の整備や電力融通の取り組みが主眼に

 ASEANの消費は中東情勢緊迫化後に減速も、概して底堅く推移

も大崩れを回避、ベトナムは堅調さを維持
 背景に各国個別要因や消費マインド悪化の歯止め
― タイは消費刺激策（6～11月）、マレーシアはインフレの抑制、ベトナムは高い賃金上昇率が消費を下支え
― 消費マインドは中東情勢緊迫化後の悪化に歯止め。もっとも、中東危機の長期化が重しに

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

3Q largely in-line; mix-driven margin dilution is well understood — Sinbon 
reported 3Q26 EPS of NT$3.79 (+1% q/q and +24% y/y), broadly in line with our 
estimate and 4% below consensus. Revenue rose 8% q/q and +28% y/y to 
NT$9.7bn, topping our/BBG forecast 4%/2%. GPM decreased 77bps to 23.8% vs. 
our estimate of 24.6%, primarily reflecting rising semi cabinet assembly sales and 
increased shipments of auto and communications. We believe the market is well 
aware of this mix-driven dilution. Opex remained at an elevated level due to 
increased product development and sampling, alongside higher freight expenses. 
Operating profits +50% y/y, missing our/BBG estimates 4%/7%, respectively.  
Solid topline momentum into 2027E led by semi — We expect semi cabinet 
assembly to remain the key growth driver, with sales potentially increasing to NT$5–
5.5bn in 2027E from NT$3–3.5bn in 2026E, led by solid demand from two major 
semi customers. While increasing contribution from this business could dilute group 
GPM, it should support sales and absolute gross profit growth. Emerging 
applications including humanoid robots and drones should provide additional

Implications — We believe the margin implications of a rising semi sales mix are well
understood by the market and leave our investment thesis intact. Our constructive
view is based on steady growth in existing businesses, supplemented by medium- to
long-term opportunities in humanoid robots, drones, and LEO satellites. We also

view Sinbon as a relatively defensive stock amid AI-related market volatility, given
its limited direct AI exposure and diversified business portfolio. The shares could
benefit from investor rotation toward companies with resilient underlying
fundamentals, while emerging applications provide additional support for the
longer-term growth outlook.

### Page 4

Sinbon (3023.TW)

Sinbon
Valuation
Our TP for Sinbon is NT$370. We value Sinbon on PE to capture the company’s earnings growth prospects. We set our target
PE at 23x, which we set at a 20% premium to the historical average of 19x since 2018. We believe 23x is justified in view of
improving fundamentals with strong momentum of industrials segment, driving a clear margin expansion and solid earnings
growth in 2026E. Applying this to our 2H26-1H27E EPS forecast, we arrive at our target price of NT$370.

Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Sinbon's share to deviate from our target price include 1) slower-than-expected growth in
non-IT/non-consumer applications; 2) Intensified competition; 3) Raw material price hike and labor cost increases; 4) FX
volatility; 5) Greater-than-expected impact from the geopolitical conflict.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Long-term strategies: Resources concentrated on ABF/ AI PCB — As we
highlighted, Unimicron has been converting BT capacity into ABF, which currently
causes some BT capacity to be idle during the transition period (negative impact will
likely last into 1H27, in our view). Thus, currently, we are less bullish on its BT GM
recovery in the near term vs other BT peers. However, we expect this conversion will

help the company to secure more ABF demand earlier considering customers
currently are still waiting for those ABF greenfield capacity expansion projects to
come in the next few years. For HDI/mSAP , Unimicron would only focus on AI
server/optical module products in the long term. To note that, the company is still
less aggressive in HDI capacity expansion given higher competition from Chinese
peers. Meanwhile, the company is also downsizing its traditional PCB capacity (i.e.,

for non-AI business) and just announced its plan to sell its PCB plant in Japan to
other players. We expect there will be more downsizing plans to come for traditional
PCB capacity.
GM expansion on track; reiterate Buy — We raise our 2026/27 /28E by 10%/5%/5%
to factor in robust GM expansion by ABF. We remain positive on its GM expansion by
ABF and also see potential upside coming from HDI and BT segments. We raise our
DCF-based TP to NT$1,550 (30x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 and reiterate our Buy

rating.
Figure 1. Unimicron – 3Q26 Results

### Page 6

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,550. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 5.0% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 14.6%. Our target price is equivalent
to 62x/30x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

months of the year), (2) new Kunshan capacity (total 1.2mn sheets/month or 21% of the
company’s current capacity) ramp up from December, and (3) the mass volume
shipment for several new AI projects (Vera Rubin + TPU v8 etc.) with a better demand
volume on the most high end M8.5/M9 CCL, we now expect EMC’s revenue to go up by
20% QoQ, and expect the GM will further expand to 40.9% (first time surpassing

For TUC, with an even faster product mix improvement speed (due to the lower base vs.

EMC) and the solid pricing hike uptrend for both high end and mid end CCL (15-20%
QoQ average pricing hike in 4Q), we expect the company to deliver a revenue growth
rate at 18% QoQ, and believe the GM will reach 40.8% (+3.9ppt QoQ) in 4Q26,
despite the slowing demand from key AI ASIC customer due to the transition period of
the ASIC projects whose capacity will be replaced by other high-end Ethernet switch
projects (also M8.5/M9 grade CCL).
Key update - EMC now plan to expand capacity to 15.25mn sheets/month from

5.85mn sheets/month capacity today, with solid demand
EMC’s BOD has approved additional capacity expansion plans extending into
2029/30E. EMC now targets year-end capacity of 8.70/10.45/11.95/15.25mn
sheets/month in 2027/28/29/30E (implying 27% 2026-30E CAGR), which is the first
time EMC announced its new capacity expansion plan after 1H28. EMC mentioned that
capacity expansion is supported by LTAs with customers who secure volumes (EMC does
not proactively initiate LTA negotiations). EMC indicated that despite its proactive

capacity expansion planned for 2027-30E, it would still be insufficient to meet with
customer demand secured under signed LTAs (demand under LTA extends at least 3Y
ahead).
EMC’s near-term capacity expansion remains intact, as the capacity expansion in
Kunshan (with additional 1.2mn sheets/month, 21% of current capacity) could
come online as early as Dec 2026 and continue through May 2027 (with new capacity
coming online every month), the expansion was originally scheduled in 2 phases, with

600k sheets/month each coming online in 1Q27 and 3Q27. The accelerated ramp-up
will offset the rescheduling of Guanyin capacity expansion, where the 300k sheet/month
expansions originally scheduled for 4Q26 have been deferred to 2H27, mainly due to
shortages of construction capacity in Taiwan as well as long equipment lead time +
Taiwan government’s strict policy on labor safety related requirement (however, we only
see delay in Taiwan, while all other plans are basically in-line or earlier than the

company’s original expectation).
Investment view - Solid margin beat + expanding margin, suggesting a stock
performance outlook; buy in the dip for both EMC & TUC
We notice that the BBG consensus for 2027 EMC/TUC GM is only at 38%/34%, and we
see upside risk to consensus EPS estimates for both EMC/TUC as the GM in 4Q could
both surpass 40-42%, without any possibility to see the GM to decrease in coming
quarters as the high-end CCL supply demand gap to further expand in coming years (see

here). As a result, we believe consensus 2027 GM could lift from 34-38% to 40-42%+ for
both companies in coming days, suggesting 10-25% EPS upward revision opportunity,
and recommend taking advantage to buy in this valuation dip (EMC/TUC only trades at
15x/12x of our bullish 2027 EPS estimate, which was far below the past 3 year range of

Moreover, EMC has mentioned all of its new capacity in coming years (capacity will

For the new CCL generation, we exect EMC to start shipment on M10 grade CCL from 
2H27, which is only for single customers’ AI GPU projects, but still implies a CCL 
migration trend in-line with our bullish view. For M9, we only see HGX R200 UBB and 
VR200 LPU adopt this solution in 2026, but see multiple new projects in 2027 will adopt 
this high ASP project (including several 1.6T ethernet switch projects, and Trainium 4/ 
TPU v9 could also see at least 50% adoption rate of the M9 CCL; moreover, the VR300 
rack should have 50%+ of its CCL content related to M9 grade CCL). We believe this new 
technology migration trend going into 2027 will further drive both companies’ ASP and 
profit in coming quarters, suggesting an even more favorable outlook for EMC/TUC’s EPS 
growth in coming years.  
Overall, we maintain our bullish view on high-end CCL players’ business outlook and 
margin expansion trend, and expect investors to start focusing on this solid margin 
expansion trend after this clean beat on GM/OPM in 3Q26; maintain Buy on 
EMC/TUC, with a new TP of NT$11,700/NT$3,460 (from NT$10,200/NT$3,010).

3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,460 is based on a 22x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(+2x STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).

研 究  報 告                
力成(6239.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
4Q26 營收維持高檔，關注高階封裝業務布局進度  
 投資評價與建議 
我們認為高階封裝業務進度如期，且為明年公司重點布局業務，整體狀況與原
先預期差異不大；我們給予公司18倍PE，為近期股價修正前之平均評價水準。 
 重點評論分析 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估
營收動能包含：(1)記憶體需求維持高檔，新業務TSV DDR5開始量產，另公司
持續因應記憶體報價上漲 ， 適時反映於封測報價 ； (2)邏輯業務中 ， FCBGA需求
維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝 ， 子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年
底擴充至2.3億顆，至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率
24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能 ：展望
2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應求至少延續至 2027年，機構估2027年
DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力；(2)邏輯業務
中，封測產業產能利用率維持高檔，持續支撐公司議價能力，而公司高階封裝
比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，如FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，子公司
Tera Probe/Terapower持續搶攻AI測試業務，另Terapower新竹新廠將於明
年貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，稅後EPS 18元。 
 投資風險 
FOPLP進展不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
3Q26本業表現大致符合預期：力成3Q26營收季增9.6%至253.3億元，毛利率24%，稅後EPS 3.44元，其中本業表
現大致符合預期，營收動能來自於記憶體需求維持穩健，子公司超豐價格調漲和客⼾需求成⻑，T e r a  
Probe/Terapower持續受惠AI/MML、HPC封測訂單支撐，而獲利方面則有價格調漲、高產能利用率(力成3Q26封
裝、測試產能利用率分別為85~90%、85%)之挹注，帶動毛利率季增2.3ppts。 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估4Q26營收動能包含：(1)記憶體業務需
求維持高檔，其中企業級SSD持續受惠於AI伺服器需求，另TSV DDR5開始量產，估月營收貢獻約1~2億元，價格
方面，公司持續因應記憶體報價上漲並適時反映於封測報價；(2)邏輯業務中，FOPLP驗證進度如期推進，FCBGA
需求維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝(78mm*88mm)，子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年底擴充至2.3億
顆 ， 另將部分不具成本優勢之產品轉移至菲律賓廠量產 ， 至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外 ，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。我們估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能：展望2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應
求至少延續至2027年，機構估2027年DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力，至於NAND
市況據機構預估明年仍有個位數的缺口或趨於平衡，僅有企業級SSD需求仍維持強勁；(2)邏輯業務則為明年最大成
⻑動能，基於整體產業產能利用率維持高檔，支撐公司議價能力，加上高階封裝比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，
包含FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，推估應用為AMD之電競晶片，子公司Tera Probe/Terapower除了既有業務持
續成⻑外，也積極搶攻美系客⼾下⼀代AI ASIC測試商機，另Terapower新竹新廠也將於明年開始貢獻營收(估月營
收貢獻約2~3億元)。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，毛利率24.5%，稅後EPS 18元。

## Document 237: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a.pdf

시장과 산업의 시차. 2026

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지

목표주가 85,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
56,700 
유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for
every nuance. Lately, there seems to have been more RBA
communication to pore over. The method of delivery has
also evolved. This has sometimes caused confusion when
tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different

tone and varying complexity.
• The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly
using speeches directed to professional audiences to
discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such
as neutral rates and financial conditions. They are also
highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more
ways than just squeezing mortgage holders.
• One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises about the future

path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running
commentary about how it is interpreting recent data. These
interpretations are themselves useful signals about what
the RBA thinks it might do.
Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks
for clues to future rate moves. They will have plenty to pore over
in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry
of fireside chats this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded

interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday. Next
Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before
Senate Estimates, just as the quarterly CPI is released. (This
timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After
the decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a
media release and press conference. More speeches will follow,
including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just

following the RBA Review but also more recently. There may
be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication
outside that certainly feels more frequent than a couple of years
ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little sparse
outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary
appearances. We have counted 58 public engagements so
far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website.
This compares with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in

2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events
were related to economics, markets and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed
as well. There are fewer set-piece speeches on policy and a
Open mouth operations
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few
years ago, there are more interviews across a wide range of

media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It was
a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the
7:30 Report ; it is hard to imagine him agreeing to an Assistant
Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general
audience. Central banks want to explain why they do what
they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising
but the household sector is not the source of the inflation.

Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes
confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief Communication Officer
highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting
media conference is designed to speak to the public. Market
participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
the primary audience. So, if you think that what the Governor
said in the media conference sounded more ‘dovish’ than
what another official says in a more market-oriented forum,

it is partly because she is trying to convey that the RBA
understands that its recent policy actions are making things
harder for many Australians. But this is in much the same way
that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It does, but that
will not stop the treatment.
A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted
material such as the minutes and set-piece speeches than in
unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be

careful with their words in unscripted settings. If the tone
differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have
reduced the pressure to make the set-piece speeches more
accessible. Research on central bank communication often
uses measures such as the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and
Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was

beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison
suggests four speeches from 2026 ( 1, 2, 3, 4) were harder
to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches
scored in the 11 to 12 range, which is high-school-level
“The RBA does not want to promise what it will
do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks. "

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and
15, which is academic or technically dense text requiring
university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-
Governor Phil Lowe also scored better on these metrics than

the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that
readability metrics do not necessarily imply better clarity if the
concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has
evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has begun to put
more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those
invisible ‘star’ variables (NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial
conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences

has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on
the full range of transmission channels from interest rates to
inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour
supply channel). This is understandable in a world of supply
shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt
they would be comforted, though, by the Deputy Governor’s

explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the
smallest of the transmission channels from interest rates
to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on
inflation. It raises the question of whether other policy tools
or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on
inflation through the other channels without hitting a minority
of households disproportionately.

We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases
to summarise the position. From ‘narrow path’ to ‘not
ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once
entrenched, a phrase can outlast its usefulness.
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record
the presentations to the Board and the members’ discussion. The
minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-
meeting statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the

Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The August 2024 minutes
were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how
sensitive the forecasts were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed
Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s
communication have not changed. It wants to explain – and
perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how
it interprets incoming data, especially inflation, the labour

market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ about future moves.
Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular
future path for interest rates. Some academic literature argues
that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high,
for that matter) independently amplifies the transmission
of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-period

interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield
curve. Central banks including the RBA have certainly tried
jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view,
though, is that the potential cost of reputational loss if things
do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a
slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides
the signal about future policy directions. This is why it was

noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour
market as ‘a little tight’ after the September meeting, even
though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We
have been noting for some time that the RBA staff have a
downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the RBA’s
oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy
Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not surprise us.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
While annual retail sales growth improved in September,
rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace of growth
is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very
weak versus history. Consumer spending continues to be

constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to
employment prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five
years into the property downturn, house prices continue to
decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep
downturn, –20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from
annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in high-tech
investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst
domestically oriented businesses as well as property’s

malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and
2028 as the contribution from net exports moderates, broad-
based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect recent
targeted support measures to be broadened and given much
greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr
in September, exceeding both market expectations and the
Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely
driven by energy, fuel prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core

inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services
inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an
immediate concern inflation, the labour market showing
resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to
4.7% in August, suggests demand risks for inflation will need
close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as
the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is released. For both events,

the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The
week after, the US mid-term elections are due.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’
attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor Hauser's fireside
chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a
follow-up rate hike at the November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook
was "materially worse" than the RBA's August forecasts.

Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having
"crystallised" ahead of the September meeting – the Middle
East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser
noted the Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices
are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in August”.
He then positioned AI and data centre developments among
an "extraordinary sequence of positive demand shocks around
the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through

into CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy
Governor noted that "inflation's been coming in at or a little
bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the
labour market". He went on to state that the staff are currently
assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement
on capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other
words, revise up their assumption for the NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of

market pricing for a follow-up November hike, from just a
20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition
today ahead of next week's Q3 CPI. For that release, we are
forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a
0.9 (3.5%yr) increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview
provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3 as
well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast.
Provided inflation prints broadly in line with our expectations,

the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.
Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3
and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to 3.7%yr. Under
the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely
watched measures of core inflation took a step higher to now
be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our
NZ economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise
the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October policy review, while

retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.
Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised  to the upside in
Q3, GDP growth accelerating from 0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting
annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on
track to achieve their 4.5–5.0% full-year growth objective.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
RBNZ now expected to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ will deliver a 25bp hike at its
policy review on 28 October. While September quarter
inflation was slightly below the RBNZ’s forecast, the
underlying detail was firm. More recently, increases in

fuel prices point to a material lift in inflation by year-end.
We expect further interest rate increases over the coming
months, with the OCR reaching a peak of 4% next year.
Softer headline inflation but firm core pressures
leave RBNZ on course to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ to raise the Official Cash Rate
by 25bp at its October policy review (previously, we had
expected a pause at this meeting). This change in view reflects
significant upside risks to near-term inflation relative to the

RBNZ’s previous forecast.
While in line with our forecast, the September quarter inflation
figures were slightly softer than the RBNZ expected, with prices
rising 3.7% over the past year (compared with the RBNZ’s
forecast of 3.9%). However, the details were less reassuring.
Excluding volatile items such as food and fuel, underlying
inflation pressures remained firm. Closely watched measures
of core inflation moved higher and now sit close to, or a little

above, the top of the RBNZ’s target band. We also saw an
increase in the number of goods recording price rises, taking
that measure back to levels seen in the wake of the pandemic.
Alongside the existing firmness in underlying inflation, the
risks for inflation over the coming quarters are firmly tilted to
the upside. That near-term upside risk has also been noted by
the Governor. Following a sharp rise in global fuel costs, we
expect inflation to reach 4.3% by the end of this year (above

the RBNZ’s September MPS forecast for +3.9%). While that is
mainly due to higher prices at the pump, businesses in sectors
such as construction are also reporting related increases in
transport and materials costs. Consistent with those trends,
we expect core inflation to remain in the upper part of the
RBNZ’s target band through 2027.
Reinforcing the case for an OCR hike at the October meeting,
the New Zealand dollar is notably weaker than the RBNZ

assumed: on a trade-weighted basis, the NZD is currently
3.6% below the RBNZ’s September projections. If sustained,
that would add to inflation pressures over the next few
quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted the RBNZ’s
medium-term outlook for inflation.
Several other developments need to be weighed against that
firmer inflation outlook. First, wholesale long-term interest rates
have risen markedly, and there has also been a more moderate

rise in some mortgage rates. The RBNZ is likely to note the role of
those increases in managing medium-term inflation risks.
In addition, the RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that
there is little evidence of a lift in businesses’ longer-term plans
for price setting at this stage in response to the rise in energy
prices (sometimes referred to as “second-round inflation
effects”). We expect the RBNZ to highlight the risk that more
persistent inflation pressures could emerge, depending on the

duration of the oil price shock.
We also expect the RBNZ to note that the labour market
remains cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Employment growth that remains weak relative to labour force
growth, subdued growth in job vacancies and low labour market
turnover are consistent with this view and could be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge that while the
global growth outlook remains solid, there are significant

downside risks from the Iran war and concerns about the
sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Balancing those concerns, we think the MPC will lift the
cash rate next week. However, a vote seems very possible as
differences in view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook has really
shifted in recent weeks.
In September, the bulk of the MPC saw upside risks to the
inflation outlook while two members (Conway and Hansen)

thought the risks were balanced. Recent developments will
not have made those seeing upside risks to inflation more
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
comfortable, while the more dovish MPC members will
likely focus on the ongoing downside risks for growth given
continued softness in house prices and the mounting pressure
on real incomes. Indeed, all MPC members saw some degree
of downside risks to growth which likely remain relevant. All

these messages should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC is likely to continue to present its decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July: a higher
OCR now provides insurance against the need for a much
higher OCR later if upside risks to medium-term inflation
materialise. We doubt the aim will be to push market interest
rates materially higher.
RBNZ to leave door open for further hikes, OCR
still expected to peak at 4% in 2027

In its forward guidance, the RBNZ is likely to leave open the option
of raising the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy
Statement, without presenting an increase as certain. While the
RBNZ will not publish an updated OCR projection at this review,
the press statement is likely to note that further increases may
be needed, depending on economic conditions. The September
projection showed the OCR eventually rising to 3.25%.
We expect the RBNZ will end up delivering another 25bp

hike in December, followed by a further hike in February.
That would take the OCR to 3.5%, roughly where the RBNZ
estimates the neutral rate to be. At that point, we expect the
RBNZ to slow the pace of rate increases (previously, we had
expected that point to be reached a little later).
We continue to expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, taking the OCR to a peak of 4.0%.
While oil prices are generating significant swings in inflation,

underlying pressures remain firm. More restrictive conditions are
likely to be needed to bring core inflation sustainably back to 2%.
Scenarios
Our central scenario, with a probability of around 60%, is that
the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp and signals that a further
increase in December remains an option, without committing to
a markedly tighter future stance than it outlined in September.
Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would see

the OCR increase to 3%, with the RBNZ placing greater
emphasis on upside risks to core and medium-term inflation.
That would suggest an upward revision to the RBNZ’s
projected OCR path in December. Downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the RBNZ leave the OCR unchanged and signal that
the medium-term inflation outlook has not changed
meaningfully from its September forecast, despite higher

near-term inflation. In this scenario, the RBNZ would
reiterate its previous forecast: a 25bp increase in December
and one further hike during 2027.
How might the election play into the decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not be
politically motivated. However, the election outcome is close.
And should it result in a Labour Party-led coalition, it seems
likely the new government would adopt the Labour Party’s

promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual mandate. That would
require the MPC to weigh the degree of labour market slack
more highly in its deliberations. As the degree of labour market
slack currently seems high, a new mandate might justify the
MPC taking more time to return inflation to the middle of the
target range (our earlier note discusses this issue). This could
figure into the deliberations of some MPC members if the
decision between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it

now is viewed as finely balanced.

Private sector credit growth came in slightly firmer than

NZ: RBNZ Official Cash Rate Review (%)
Oct 28, Last: 2.75, Westpac f/c: 3.00, Mkt f/c: 3.00
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. Data since September show a higher
outlook for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP starting

point, but increased risks to domestic consumption and
residential investment. On balance, the medium-term inflation

NZ: Oct ANZ Business Confidence (index)
Oct 29, Last: 51.9
Firms remained optimistic in the September survey of business
confidence, with both the activity and inflation measures
broadly holding steady. However, it’s likely that most responses
were submitted in the early part of the month, while fuel prices
took a sharp step higher from the second half of September. The

survey comes too late for the RBNZ’s October policy review, but
evidence about the breadth of cost and price pressures could
have a bearing on the RBNZ’s thinking about the total amount of
policy tightening needed in the months ahead.
NZ: Sep Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Oct 28, Last: 0.2, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has been gradually ticking higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The

weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result for
September – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase. Given the
history of this measure, however, both this month and the
previous month are likely to be revised down a little from their
initial release.

Oct FOMC meeting
Oct 27-28, Last: 3.875%, Mkt f/c: 3.875%, WBC f/c: 3.875%
Core CPI inflation and producer price inflation both surprised
to the upside in September, and the price of Brent oil has
risen back to around USD110. Underlying momentum in
the economy also remains robust, although employment

disappointed in the latest update. Given the concerns shown
by Committee members over price risks and their collective
resolve to return annual inflation to target, the above data
could easily justify a follow-up hike at the October meeting.
That said, Committee members who have spoken recently
have shown patience with respect to assessing the economy
and positioing policy. The market has responded, effectively
pricing out the chance of an October hike in favour of a

December move, which is also our expectation.
US Q3 GDP
Oct 29, %ann’d, Last: 2.2%, Mkt f/c: 3.0%, WBC f/c: 3.1%
The US economy continues to grow at a pace well above trend.
This outperformance is almost entirely due to the strength of
investment in data centres and other AI related assets. The
pipeline for this activity remains strong, limiting near-term
risks to the downside.
Abstracting from AI investment’s contribution, other business
investment and residential investment is weak, weighed down

by high term interest rates and uncertainty related to trade
policy. Household consumption growth is volatile month-to-
month, but the trend is solid. Absent a marked deterioration
in the labour market, which is not our expectation, this
momentum is likely to persist, as wealth continues to grow and
household income gains neutralises inflation. Come 2027 and
2028, growth is expected to slow to trend, but not below.

TUẦN 12-16/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận:

- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn

## Document 377: Marvell收购Lumentum：“芯片 + 光学”的首次深度闭环，“既当裁判又当运动员”与光通信的“赢家通吃”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Marvell收购Lumentum：“芯片 + 光学”的首次深度闭环，“既当裁判又当运动员”与光通信的“赢家通吃”.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Marvell 拟收购 Lumentum ，可能打通 DSP 、激光器到光模块的产业链。 Cignal AI 两位分析
师认为， AI 时代光学组件价值⼤幅提升，垂直整合有望创造更⾼利润。但 Marvell 也将⾯临
⼀个棘⼿问题：既向光模块⼚商供应芯⽚，⼜可能成为它们的竞争对⼿。
摘要：
10 ⽉ 28 ⽇，彭博报道称， Marvell 正与光学器件制造商 Lumentum 进⾏深⼊谈判，考虑通过全股
票交易收购后者。报道当时给出的 Lumentum 市值约为 830 亿美元， Marvell 约为 2210 亿美元。
这笔潜在交易引发了⼀个值得关注的问题：⼀家以定制芯⽚和⾼速互连⻅⻓的公司，为什么要
⼤举进⼊激光器和光学器件制造领域？
10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，半导体⾏业播客 Semi Doped 邀请市场研究机构 Cignal AI 的两位分析师 Andrew
Schmitt 和 Scott Wilkinson 讨论这笔交易。两⼈的判断是， Marvell 希望通过垂直整合，把 DSP 、

激光器和光学组件结合起来，形成更完整的产品体系。
如果交易完成， Marvell 可能成为少数同时覆盖 DSP 、激光器和光模块多个环节的企业。但这也
会带来新的竞争关系：它⼀边向光模块⼚商供应芯⽚，⼀边可能进⼊这些客⼾的市场。
⼀、从 DSP 到激光器、光模块： Marvell 想打通整条链
主持⼈提出， Marvell 收购 Lumentum 后，可能成为⼀家同时拥有 DSP 、激光器和光模块业务的
公司。 Andrew Schmitt 认为，最接近这⼀模式的先例是 Acacia ，但 Marvell 与 Lumentum 的组合
在业务规模和覆盖范围上可能更进⼀步。
他⾸先澄清， Marvell 并不缺激光器供应。公司⽬前主要为 ZR 相⼲模块采购可调谐激光器，与多
家供应商保持着良好关系。收购 Lumentum 的核⼼驱动⼒，是垂直整合。
Andrew 认为，在技术快速变化的市场中，企业需要同时掌握芯⽚和光学技术，才能更好地控制
产品开发和供应链。两类技术放在同⼀家公司内部，可以协同设计，做出性能更好的产品，也

能通过销售更完整的⽅案获得更多⽑利。
Lumentum ⾃⾝已经有类似的业务组合。 Andrew 举例，公司此前收购 Cloud Light ，进⼊基于硅
光技术的数据通信光模块市场。 Cloud Light ⽣产的光模块使⽤ Lumentum ⾃⼰的激光器，同时还
需要从 Marvell 、 Broadcom 等公司采购 PAM4 DSP 。
在⼀只光模块中，激光器和 DSP ⼏乎构成了最主要的价值部分，剩下的环节主要是组装。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这意味着， Marvell 与 Lumentum 的业务本来就存在上下游联系。收购完成后， Marvell 有机会将
原本需要外部采购的关键组件纳⼊内部体系，进⼀步整合 DSP 、激光器和光学引擎。
Andrew 认为，这种模式的价值还体现在利润上。
过去，芯⽚公司的⽑利率可能达到 50% ，光学组件⼚商却只有 25% ⾄ 30% 。芯⽚公司⾃然不愿
意收购⼀家⽑利率更低的企业，担⼼拉低⾃⾝利润率。
但他表⽰，如今光学组件⾏业的⽑利率已经从约 25% 上升⾄接近 50% 。 Lumentum 在 200G
EML 、光电路交换机（ OCS ）和⾼功率激光器等产品上拥有稀缺技术，议价能⼒也明显增强。
“ 如果你能赚到更多的钱，只不过这些钱占总收⼊的⽐例低⼀些，那⼜有什么关系？ ”
Andrew 认为，投资者过去过于看重⽑利率百分⽐，忽视了企业能够创造的⽑利额。将组件整合
成更完整的产品，即使合并后的⽑利率⽐例有所变化，也可能创造更多利润。
他还提到， Marvell 六七年前可能只需约 30 亿美元就能收购 Lumentum ，如今潜在交易规模可能

达到当年的 30 倍。这是他的回忆和估算，却也反映出光学组件在 AI 时代的价值变化。
⼆、既当 “ 裁判 ” ⼜当 “ 运动员 ” ： Marvell 可能与客⼾竞争
垂直整合会让 Marvell 获得更完整的产品线，也会改变它与客⼾的关系。
Scott Wilkinson 指出， Marvell ⽬前的相⼲ DSP 被⼤量光模块⼚商采⽤。以 400G 相⼲模块为例，
除 Acacia 、 Ciena 、 Nokia 以及部分中国⼚商外，很多企业都会采购 Marvell 的 DSP 。
如果 Marvell 收购 Lumentum ，进⼀步进⼊光学引擎和模块市场，它就可能与这些原有客⼾直接
竞争。
这正是交易中⼀个微妙的地⽅： Marvell 既是产业链上游的芯⽚供应商，也可能成为下游产品的
参与者。客⼾难免会考虑，未来是否还要继续依赖⼀家与⾃⼰竞争的公司提供关键芯⽚。
主持⼈ Austin 提出了⼀个直接的问题：如果客⼾因此转向 Broadcom 、 Credo 等其他 DSP 供应

商， Marvell 是否愿意牺牲部分芯⽚市场份额，换取光模块业务？
Andrew 认为，这是⼀笔可以计算的商业账：
“ 通过垂直整合，我能够获得⽐单独销售零部件更多的价值，即使这意味着我可能会少卖⼀些零
部件。 ”
Scott 也提醒，真正需要观察的是 Marvell 如何管理收购后的业务。 Lumentum 本来就向外部客⼾
销售激光器，也经营部分光模块业务。收购完成后， Marvell 未必会把这些产能全部留给⾃⼰。
Andrew 则认为，客⼾是否流失，取决于 Marvell 能否给原有 DSP 客⼾和 Lumentum 的光学客⼾⾜
够的信⼼，让他们相信原有供货关系不会受到影响。
这也是垂直整合的代价：企业获得了更多产业链控制权，同时也可能让客⼾重新寻找供应商。
三、光通信的 “ 赢家通吃 ” ：先发优势不如百万级交付

### Page 3

除了垂直整合，两位分析师还谈到了光通信⾏业另⼀个鲜明的竞争规律：新技术标准的市场最
终往往由少数⼚商占据。
Andrew 把这⼀现象称为 “IEEE 彩票 ” 。
每当⾏业推出⼀项新标准，通常会有五六家公司同时开发产品。⼏年之后，真正占据⼤部分出
货量的往往只剩两家公司，合计拿下 80% ⾄ 90% 的市场份额。
因此，率先推出产品固然重要，但能否抓住市场形成初期的机会，才决定了企业能否成为最终
赢家。
Scott 进⼀步指出，当前 AI 光通信市场的竞争尤其看重产能。
“ 如果你第⼀个进⼊市场，却只能⽣产⼀万个，实际上没有太⼤意义。如果你第⼆个进⼊市场，
却能够⽣产⼀百万个，那么订单就会落到你⼿⾥。 ”
他以中国⼚商 Eoptolink （光迅科技）为例。这家公司在 800G 以太⽹光模块市场进⼊时间稍晚于
部分竞争对⼿，却凭借产能优势成为全球主要供应商之⼀。 Scott 认为， 1.6T 产品也正在出现类
似的竞争局⾯。
这也是 Lumentum 受到关注的原因。 Scott 提到，公司过去⼏年持续扩⼤磷化铟（ InP ）制造能

⼒，产能经历了多轮翻倍式扩张，但市场需求增⻓更快，仍然难以满⾜所有客⼾需求。
Lumentum 的优势还在于拥有⼀些竞争对⼿难以迅速复制的产品。 Andrew 特别提到 200G
EML 、 OCS 和⾼功率激光器。
他提醒，很多公司都可以宣称⾃⼰能够开发出类似产品，但真正的考验是能否从⽣产⼏百件扩
⼤到五万件、甚⾄⼀百万件。 “ 做出⼀百个和做出⼀百万个，完全是两回事。 ”
AI 时代，这种规模优势被进⼀步放⼤。 Andrew 认为，光学组件的价值也发⽣了变化：在传统电
信⽹络中，激光器主要帮助运营商增加传输容量；在 AI 数据中⼼中， 1.6T 互联可能让 AI 集群训练
速度提⾼两到三倍。
当⼀项光学技术能够显著提升整个计算集群的效率，客⼾愿意为它⽀付的价格⾃然会发⽣变
化。
四、 Nvidia 会不会加⼊竞购？
访谈最后，两位分析师讨论了 Nvidia 是否可能成为潜在买家。
Nvidia 此前已分别向 Lumentum 和 Coherent 投资 20 亿美元，并与两家公司建⽴战略合作。

Andrew 认为， Nvidia 同样有垂直整合的动⼒：此前收购 Mellanox ，就是为了把⽹络技术纳⼊⾃
⾝ GPU 系统。如果继续向光学组件和 OCS 领域延伸，也符合这⼀思路。
Andrew 还提出⼀种可能性： Marvell 与 Lumentum 的谈判消息如果是有意泄露，或许是在向其他
潜在买家释放信号，最终引发竞购战。
不过，他明确表⽰⾃⼰没有交易内幕，这只是个⼈推测。 Nvidia 此前的投资和采购合作，也不能
证明它有意参与收购。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

1 条评论
对 Marvell 来说，这笔交易的核⼼考验最终仍是如何平衡两件事：通过整合芯⽚与光学技术获得
更⾼的整体价值，同时避免原有客⼾因为竞争关系⽽转向其他供应商。
Andrew Schmitt 的判断是，光学组件已经成为 AI 基础设施中价值更⾼的环节，芯⽚公司收购光
学企业的经济逻辑正在改变。 Scott Wilkinson 则提醒，在光通信这个容易出现 “ 赢家通吃 ” 的市
场，产能和规模决定最终份额。
Marvell 想打通从芯⽚到光学的产业链，能否同时守住客⼾、技术和市场份额，将决定这场整合
究竟能创造多⼤价值。

台燿 6274 TT ( 電子–電子零組件 )

逆風燿進，屹立不搖 
投資與評價 
1. 台燿 3Q26 在泰國廠進度提前開始營收貢獻下，營運創歷史新高；4Q26 預
期將進行 CCL 產品價格調漲，營運將續創歷史新高。 
2. 台燿 26 年泰國廠區布局順利開出，受益於漲價策略帶動營運將逐季增成
長，27 年有望持續提升高階應用佔比，同時持續擴充產能供應，中長期營
運持續成長，預估 26 年 EPS 41.93 元、27 年 EPS 86.20 元。 
3. 考量台燿受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，新產能將持續開出，
26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升 ，調升 27 年獲利預估，目標價上修至 1,900
元(22x 修正後 27(F)PER)，並將投資評等由買進轉為強力買進。

重點更新 
台燿 3Q26 泰國廠進度提前且轉盈，營運創歷史新高 
台燿 3Q26 受益於泰國廠 1-2 期因學習曲線提升，提前一個月至 7M26 開始貢
獻，爬坡期順利，並且持續開出產能貢獻之下，泰國廠順利轉虧為盈且幅度已超
上半年所有虧損，加上來自美系網通交換器、ASIC 專案持續提升貢獻，帶動台
燿 3Q26 營收季增 25.5%達 179.49 億元，優於原估之 173.16 億元(+21.1% 
QoQ)3.7%。毛利率方面，泰國廠因產能爬坡期順利進行，營運轉虧為盈，帶動
毛利率大幅提升 7.2 個百分點至 36.9%優於原預估 33.3%之 3.6 個百分點，且
因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得宜，營業費用率下滑至 5.9%，優於原
預估 6.2%，帶動營業利益達 55.68 億元(+65.8% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 64.7%
至 38.57 億元，EPS 12.88 元，營運達歷史新高。 
4Q26 CCL 產品價格持續調漲，預期台燿營運將續創新高 
展望 4Q26，泰國廠在產能順開之下，將再度開出10 萬張 CCL 產能，因應來自
終端的強勁需求；另外，目前上游材料包含玻纖布、銅箔等持續進行產品價格調
整，且在高階材料越發緊缺之下，M7/M8 等級之 CCL 產品將較過去有這較大
幅度的調漲，故上修台燿 3Q26 營收預估 6.4%至 207.53 億元(+15.6% QoQ)，
受益於高階 AI 應用業務佔比持續攀升與漲價挹注，毛利率預期將提升 1.6 個百
分點至 38.5%；且因營收規模擴大，營業費用率隨之下滑至 5.8%，帶動營業淨
利達 67.94 億元(+22.0% QoQ)，隨之上調預估稅後淨利 25.3%至 50.99 億元
(+32.2% QoQ)，EPS 17.03 元，營運將續創新高。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
台燿 3Q26 泰國廠進度提前且轉盈，營運創歷史新高 
台燿 3Q26 受益於泰國廠 1-2 期因學習曲線提升 ，提前一個月至 7M26 開始貢獻，爬坡期順利 ，
並且持續開出產能貢獻之下，泰國廠順利轉虧為盈且幅度已超上半年所有虧損，加上來自美系網
通交換器、ASIC 專案持續提升貢獻，帶動台燿 3Q26 營收季增 25.5%達 179.49 億元，優於原估
之 173.16 億元(+21.1% QoQ)3.7%。 
毛利率方面，受惠泰國廠營運轉虧為盈，帶動毛利率大幅提升 7.2 個百分點至 36.9%優於原預估
33.3%之 3.6 個百分點，且因營收規模擴大， 再加上銷售費用控管得宜 ， 營業費用率下滑至 5.9%，
優於原預估 6.2%，帶動營業利益達 55.68 億元(+65.8% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 64.7%至 38.57
億元，EPS 12.88 元，營運達歷史新高。

展望 4Q26，泰國廠在產能順開之下 ， 將再度開出10 萬張 CCL 產能 ， 因應來自終端的強勁需求 ；

M7/M8 等級之 CCL 產品將較過去有這較大幅度的調漲，故上修台燿 3Q26 營收預估 6.4%至
207.53 億元(+15.6% QoQ)，受益於高階 AI 應用業務佔比持續攀升與漲價挹注，毛利率預期將

泰國廠區放量貢獻與高階產品提升，預期台燿 26 年營運創新高 
因應眾多 PCB 板廠於泰國廠產能將於 26 年開出，台燿持續擴大於泰國地區之布局。伴隨泰國廠
1-2 期提前至 7M26 上線，4Q26 將進入全面量產階段，專注於衛星地面板、ASIC 伺服器專案
等產品，伴隨產能逐步釋放，26 年底總產能將達 260 萬張，有望進一步帶動台燿於高階 AI 伺服
器、交換器以及衛星相關板材之供應份額。此外，觀察目前上游原料價格走勢持續飆漲，4Q26
仍將持續進行成本轉嫁調漲策略。 
故上修台燿整體營收預估 3.1%至 630.56 億元(+107.8% YoY)。在漲價效益、產品組合優化以及
泰國廠轉營帶動下，調高毛利率預估至 33.9%至(+11.2 ppts YoY)，並且受益於營運規模擴大與
費用控管， 調降營業費用率預估至 6.1%(-2.3 ppts YoY)，營業利益為 175.48 億元(+304.0% YoY)，
帶動稅後淨利達 125.58 億元(+248.6% YoY)，EPS 42.29 元，獲利翻倍成長且營運創新高。

預期台燿 27 年提升更多高階應用占比，營運將再度創高 
展望 27 年，AI 相關需求在更高傳輸速度及訊號低損耗條件下，終端 PCB 規格持續向上靠攏，同
時板層數也持續往 30 甚至 40 層以上研發，帶動整體 CCL 價量齊揚。台燿將受益於 ASIC 客戶
新一代平台的採用，與 AI 伺服器 CPU 板材規格提升至 M8，以及於高階交換器 800G/ 1.6T 新
客戶進展順利下，帶動台燿高階產品組合持續增加。此外，公司亦有望受惠美系衛星供應商 V3
新一代衛星和 800G/ 1.6T 光模塊之採用。來自終端需求遍地開花之下，台燿 27 年亦將持續擴
充產能，預計於中國常熟及泰國各新增 60 萬張，合計120 萬張產能規劃，年底將達 380 萬張總
產能。故上修台燿 27 年營收預估 2.7%至 1,081,29 億元(+71.5% YoY)。 
伴隨台燿持續提升高端應用佔比，再度調高毛利率預估至 40.0%(+6.0 ppts YoY)，且受益於營收
規模持續大幅提升，調低營業費用率至 6.0%(-0.2 ppts YoY)，預期營業利益將達 367.68 億元
(+109.5% YoY)，稅後淨利季增 105.6%至 258.13 億元，EPS 86.20 元，獲利仍將大幅成長且營
運再度創高。

### Page 4

高階產品出貨比重將持續拉高，帶動台燿 26 及 27 年營運續創新高 
台燿 26 年泰國廠區布局順利開出，配合客戶大量終端需求，已於 3Q26 轉虧為盈，加上漲價策
略帶動下營運將季季增，27 年有望持續提升高階應用佔比，如 AI CPU 、ASIC、Switch 等相關
專案，同時持續擴充產能供應，中長期營運持續成長，預期至 27 年獲利仍保持倍增之成長，預
估 26 年 EPS 42.29 元、27 年 EPS 86.20 元。 
目前台燿股價為 1,375 元，約為 15.5x FY27E EPS ，位於近 3 年 PER 參考區間 5~30x 中間偏下
之位置。考量台燿受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，且近兩年擴產計畫將順利、穩定開
出貢獻，將有利公司爭取更多淺在訂單，26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升。近期針對台燿相關 AI
專案之進展，研究部認為下世代平台之規格尚未訂定，公司仍持續與相關客戶密切合作，加上台
燿近兩年擴產計畫迅速且明確，或將有望拿下更多新專案，搶占相關同業份額。故調升 27 年獲
利預估，但考量未來兩季本業成長增速趨緩，將本益比由近上緣之 25 倍略微調低至中間偏上 22
倍，目標價由 1,975 元(25x 修正前 27(F)PER)上修至 1,900 元(22x 修正後 27(F)PER)，並將投資
評等由買進轉為強力買進。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
台燿科技 TUC（6274 TT） 成立於 1974 年，總部位於台灣新竹縣竹北市，初期以光學玻璃製造 
為主要業務 ，1997 年成功轉型跨入銅箔基板 （Copper Clad Laminate, CCL） 與黏合片 （Prepreg） 
之生產領域，2003 年正式在台掛牌上櫃。台燿為全球高階 CCL 材料領導廠之一，核心產品涵蓋 
高頻、高速、低損耗及高耐熱等系列，廣泛應用於AI 伺服器、網通設備、汽車電子、消費性電子 
與高速資料傳輸板材。公司並提供多層壓合代工（Mass Lamination）服務，具備從原材製造到 
加工整合的一站式能力，形成完整的供應鏈垂直整合優勢。3Q26 營收佔比：ELL&SLL(M7/M8) 
38%、VLL&LL(M4/M6) 28%、HDI&N-LL 19%、Others 15%。

台燿 ESG 政策 
台燿科技秉持「誠信經營、創新永續」的企業核心，積極回應全球淨零減碳趨勢。2024 年全面 
導入 ISO 14064-1 溫室氣體盤查制度 ， 完成第三方查證並據此制定短中長期減量目標 ， 以 2021 
年為基準年，規劃至 2025 年減碳 20%、2030 年減碳 42%、2050 年持續每年再降 2%。公
司同 時持續精進節能管理，2024 年推行多項節電專案，節電量達 21 萬度，能源密集度降至 
19.78 (MWh/百萬元營收)，溫室氣體排放總量 108,554 公噸 CO₂e、強度 11.65 。除推動節
能外， 台燿也積極落實廢棄物源頭減量與回收管理，回收率維持 75％ 以上，並推動綠電採購
與碳費應 對策略。公司依 TCFD 架構揭露氣候風險，將氣候議題納入董事會監督範疇，展現與
國際減碳目 標接軌的決心。 台燿推動永續供應鏈管理，強化上游合作夥伴的 ESG 韌性。2024 
年共完成 26 家主要供應商的 環境與人權稽核，合格率 100％，並要求所有供應商簽署社會責
任與環保承諾書，確保不使用衝 突礦產及有害化學物質。公司已通過  RBA 責任商業聯盟稽核
且未有重大缺失，持續推動供應商 進行溫室氣體盤查與減碳培訓，並逐步導入永續採購制度。
為落實環保製造，台燿全廠導入 ISO 14001 環境管理系統與污染防治方案，強化廢水、廢氣與
噪音監控，確保所有放流水與排放皆符 合法規。透過持續改善與外部驗證，台燿建立透明、低碳
且高效率的供應鏈體系，穩固其作為高 階 CCL 材料領導廠的綠色形象。 
在人本管理方面，台燿視員工為企業最重要的資產。公司全面推行 ISO 45001 職業安全衛生管 
理系統，定期舉辦消防演練、健康檢查及安全教育，2024 年無重大職災事件，勞安指標持續改 
善。人權訓練涵蓋新進人員 100％、在職員工 96％，並透過性平與反歧視政策保障同工同酬。 
2024 年非主管職員工薪資平均 NT$951,877，中位數 NT$858,478，較前一年均提升。公司同 
時持續優化福利與人才培育制度，員工人均訓練時數  9.7 小時，女性員工與男性員工訓練時數  
相當。台燿致力打造安全、健康、包容的職場環境，讓員工能在穩定中成長，於工作中實現自我 
價值。 在公司治理層面，台燿以「誠信、透明、問責」為原則，長期維持公司治理評鑑前 6–20
％ 水準。 董事會中獨立董事席次超過半數，並已將 ESG 績效納入高階主管考核機制。公司制
定道德行為 準則與檢舉機制，嚴禁賄賂、貪腐及任何形式的強迫勞動，並推動供應鏈衝突礦產
盡職調查。資 訊安全方面，台燿建置完善的資安防護系統與個資保護管理制度，2024 年未發生
任何資安事件， 員工資安訓練達 691 人次。透過董事會與內控監督機制，台燿持續強化治理透
明度與風險管理 韌性，奠定企業長期穩健經營的基石。 台燿長期深耕在地公益，2024 年投入
社會關懷、教育與環保活動，推動員工志工、節能減碳倡 議及社區回饋計畫。公司定期舉辦淨灘
與捐血活動，並捐助地方教育與弱勢團體，實踐企業公民 責任。未來，台燿將以 「誠信治理、
綠色製造、人本共榮」 為三大支柱，持續朝 2050 淨零排 放目標邁進，推動電子材料產業的永
續創新，致力成為全球客戶信賴的高階 CCL 永續夥伴。

## Document 503: 【新興亞洲攻略】台灣央行利率連十凍，川習會前瞻， 高利環境與期中選舉下的新亞操作

台灣央行利率連十凍，川習會前瞻，
高利環境與期中選舉下的新亞操作
分析師／吳雨桑
研究範疇／新興市場 / 新興亞洲 / 中國 / 香港/ 台灣
新興亞洲投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/24
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望
中國兩年期殖利率 1.25% 景氣下行壓力持續，本行仍預期接下來有降準50bp空間。
新興市場股 中立 油價高檔拉鋸，主要國家央行貨幣政策偏向緊縮，債務堆壘隱憂揮之不去，高利環境下，兼具基本面穩健與政策操作有空間的新亞、拉美相對正向。
新興亞洲 中立 新興亞洲中相對看好台灣、韓國、東協與印度，AI推論的供不應求，適時留意評價面空間循序參與台韓投資契機，印度有評價面保護優勢。

中國A 中立 中國景氣K型化加劇，投資方向維持選股不選市，聚焦受惠AI相關供應鏈兼國產替代政策扶植的科技族群、稀缺原物料及高附加出口價值等產業。
香港 中立 削價競爭雖告尾聲但內需乏力影響獲利，並關注資本支出轉為獲利進程。
台灣 正向 長線台股AI供應鏈握技術進程優勢與漲價潮，擇評價面分批布局台股。

《投資關鍵-修正過後資金重拾AI投資議題》
9月全球股市歷經主要國家貨幣政策轉趨鷹派，市場
對政府債務堆壘的疑慮 ，主要國家公債殖利高檔拉
鋸影響對風險性資產的偏好 。惟歷經適度修正後 ，
市場重拾對AI投資主題的信心 ，台韓股市肩負重要
AI基礎建設的供應鏈要角 ，評價調整後重新吸引資
金進駐，在全球股市震盪之際中表現相對亮眼。

重點摘要
3
• 8月中國經濟數據仍顯示
外貿強 ，內需疲軟的景
象。除反應信心不足 、
內需疲軟外 ，反內卷的
制約以及政策傾斜高端
科技是主因。
• 中國經濟現階段來自價
格為主的出口貢獻 ，景
氣越趨依賴全球科技週
期，內需疲軟將視財政
執行力道進程才能緩解
接下來的景氣下行壓力 。
《投資關鍵-中國經濟K型化情勢依舊》
縱觀8月中國經濟數據仍顯示外貿強，內需疲軟的景
象。中國8月出口增速從前月23.9%回升至25%，究
其細項，科技相關以名目計算年增速續保高速 ，如
自動資料處理機器零件年增76.5%， 儲存組件年增
159.2%，印刷電路板年增 44.4%與積體電路年增
129.8%。惟若以數量計算年增大多回落，顯示科技

8月出口增速佳來自價格貢獻。另一留意低端消費出
口也見改善，可觀察歐美消費旺季的挹注力道。
8月零售銷售持續疲軟年增回落至 0.4%，汽車零售
年衰退幅度擴大至-18.5%是主要拖累，零組件價格
上揚帶動通訊設備零售增27.3%。
8月實質工業生產年增略好預期至 5.2%，科技相關
保持較高能見度 ，通訊、電腦及其他電子設備年增
17.2%，但如汽車 、粗鋼與水泥較反應內需表現黯
淡，反內卷政策制約太陽能板等生產表現 。固定資
產投資年衰退幅度擴大至 -7.2%，房地產投資雙位
數衰退趨勢不變 ，製造業投資年減 -2.3%，我們認
為除反應信心不足 、內需疲軟外 ，反內卷的制約以
及政策傾斜高端科技是主因。
總體來看，中國經濟現階段來自價格為主的出口貢
獻，景氣越趨依賴全球科技週期 ，內需疲軟將視財
政執行力道進程才能緩解接下來的景氣下行壓力。
出口雖穩健，但內需與投資乏力，產能利用不振
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/22。

上限至 7成，同時也刪除

《投資關鍵-台灣連十季利率不動 ，但升息壓力增 》
台灣央行第三季理監事會議宣布重貼現率、擔保放
款融通利率與短期融通利率維持不變在 2.0%、
2.375%和4.25%；可轉讓定期存單 (NCD) 28天期、
91 天期 、 182 天 期 也 維 持1.215% 、 1.275% 和
1.395% 不變。2024年以來，台灣央行持續保持利
率十連凍紀錄。
不動產信用管制部分 ，由於截至7月底，全體銀行
不動產貸款集中度續降至34.44%，低於2020年12

月底(第1度調整信用管制時點 )之 36.64%)，因此
調升自然人第 2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限至 7成。
同時也刪除購地貸款有關切結於一定期間內動工興
建之規定，相關規範回歸銀行依授信實務辦理。
資料來源：台灣央行，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/18。
銀行不動產貸款集中度低於首次信用管制時集中度

2026 年 經 濟 成 長 率 由9.45% 至 11.48% 。 展望

預測經濟成長 5.82%。
物價方面，氣候影響預期蔬果等食物類價格居高 ，

央行目標
相較歐美日韓主要國家央行相繼升息的舉措 ，台
灣央行維持利率連十凍理由如下 :首先，台灣央行
認為此波通膨壓力主要 是因供給測問題所致 ，因
此不宜採取升息舉措應對。其次，預估2027年通
膨可望下降;再者，台灣經濟成長貢獻來自外需 ，
但傳統產業出口動能溫吞 ，且內需成長尚不穩固 ，
景氣復甦不均現象存。最後，台灣央行強調2024

6
• 本行認為台灣景氣動能佳

年以來相較各國重返寬鬆貨幣政策列車 ，台灣依
舊維持基準利率 2%，2024/7各類新台幣存款準
備率調升 1碼。另觀察台灣 7月M2年增速 7.42%
(今年以來6.6%)雖高於政策目標區間 2.5%~6.5%，
但低於17.8%的名目 GDP 增長率，意味貨幣需求
增長速度超過貨幣供應量 ，因此台灣貨幣政策環
境已偏緊縮，所以並不需要立即升息。
展望後市，隨AI應用擴散至推論 、代理及終端裝
置推升算力需求 ，AI投資建設的暢旺驅動相關硬
體升級與用量成長 ，台灣企業涵蓋重要 AI供應鏈
節點，強勁出口引領經濟成長。

觀察AI科技紅利也逐步外溢其他產業 ，非科技如
機械出口逐步加溫 。相關半導體投資擴產需求積
極，投資成長也助力台灣經濟 ，內需也因股市財
富效應帶動。台灣景氣動能佳且增長更加均衡 ，
形成經濟的正向循環。
資料來源：主計處，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/08/07。
AI科技紅利外溢，消費與投資對景氣貢獻逐步擴大
本行認為台灣經濟環境已有宏觀的升息條件 ，而
從央行聲明觀察對於左右貨幣政策的觀察事項 ，
分別為國內通膨發展 ，主要經濟體貨幣政策緊縮
程度，國內金融情勢 、其他不確定性如 AI應用前
景。相較先前，央行將通膨發展情勢列為首要抉
擇貨幣政策指標。本行認為近期台灣的通膨情勢
對台灣經濟成長yoy貢獻 %

並非主要來自供給測壓力，主要是考量核心CPI 已

的時間最快在2027農曆春節（2月4日至10日）前
後，抑或在元宵節（2月20日）前。而根據央行預
估1Q27整體物價增速仍高於 2%，若疊加現金補
貼的加持 ，不排除物價增幅將更高於原預期水準 。
其次央行聲明第二個觀察重點 : 主要經濟體貨幣政
策緊縮程度。美歐日央行9月已升息一碼，美聯準
會年底尚有升息一碼的可能性 ，韓國央行 7、8月
連續性升息後，11月與1Q27都有可能再度升息。
多國相繼貨幣政策緊縮態勢 ，或增加台灣央行貨
幣政策抉擇的壓力 。而若台灣基準利率與他國利
差擴大 ，也可能引發資金外流 ，也不利央行關心
的金融情勢發展 。因此綜合來看 ，本行預期 12月
央行理監事會議有可能決議升息半碼至2.125%。
《投資關鍵-聚焦穩健基本面有望抵禦高利環境》
展望後市，美財政部有意擴大美債回購規模緩和債

務壓力，美聯準會9月升息一碼至3.75%-4.0%，年
底不排除還有一碼的升息空間，但從通膨與景氣預
估路徑來看，美聯準會的升息舉措並非典型的升息
循環，而是「不承諾路徑的風險管理式升息」。
回到對新興亞洲貨幣政策路徑來看，或許促使部分
新興亞洲央行政策光譜往緊縮端推移，但不改變原
具降息需求新亞國家的寬鬆方向，整體新亞國家貨
幣政策走向分歧。更重要的是決定新興亞洲貨幣政
策的考量點，如台灣與韓國主要是因應景氣回溫而
貨幣政策緊縮，但像無財政補貼物價致緊縮貨幣政
策國家如菲律賓，再者還有經濟成動能尚不穩固或
通膨可控，貨幣政策按兵不動的如中國、泰國等。

熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
市場投資展望
重點摘要
本月關鍵圖表
臉書估值修復但指數上行空間仍
因谷歌承壓
分析師／張偉漢
研究範疇／通訊服務
市場投資展望
《臉書 Muse 點燃追趕行情，谷歌困於模型商品化
與旗艦空窗》
臉書代理式 AI 產品 Muse 迅速爆紅，推動股價單日
創 13 個月來最佳表現。市場開始將臉書從「AI 資
本支出大戶」重新定位為「具備自有 AI 爆款產品的
平台」。月底臉書乘勝進軍企業 AI 平台。然而，AI
代理競爭仍在初期 ，臉書雖挾分發優勢 ，但變現仍
待觀察。谷歌則在法務與搜尋基本面接連傳出利多 。
然而，OpenAI 與 Anthropic 接連大砍 API 價格導
致 AI 推理商品化疑慮，加上旗艦模型 Gemini 4 仍

遲遲未推出 ，谷歌股價因此持續承壓 。資本支出強
度攀升導致的 ROIC 走弱的核心敘事尚未見到反轉 ，
因此通訊服務仍維持中性看待。
• 9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI
代理 Muse，正面回應市場持
續關注的變現疑問。
• 臉書近期也成立企業 AI 平台，
將代理人技術延伸至企業端。
• 臉書成功回應市場對臉書有無
AI 應用產品的疑慮。但投入回
收成效仍待進一步驗證。
• 谷歌整合式廣告業務營運未受
結構性衝擊，搜尋有自 AI 競
爭者收復失土的跡象。
• 但谷歌在 AI 模型層仍面臨 AI
兩大壓力，不利市場信心。
1
資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
通訊服務 中立 通訊服務主成分臉書近期推出消費端 AI 代理 Muse 與企業 AI 平台，
成功回應市場對於「臉書有沒有 AI 產品」的疑問，然後續 AI 投資變
現結果仍待逐步驗證。谷歌流失在廣告與搜尋業務迎來好消息，然在
AI 模型端面臨雙重逆風，不利市場信心。本行預期市場仍將聚焦檢視
資本支出效率與變現進度。靜候 10 月財報結果，維持中立評等。

資料來源：Koyfin、Capital IQ，2026/09/29；中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/29。

消費端與企業端同步開展 P.2

重點摘要

• 9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI
代理 Muse，正面回應市場持
續關注的變現疑問。

平台，以圖將代理人技術從消

AI 應用產品的疑慮。但投入回

2
《臉書 AI 投資變現路徑初具雛形，消費端與企業端
同步開展》
9 月臉書推出面向消費端的 AI 代理 Muse，正面回
應市場持續關注的「臉書能否以自有 AI 產品在 40
億用戶基礎上變現」的疑問。Muse 是能代替用戶
執行比價、訂購、換約等具體任務的個人代理人。
上市後迅速登上美國 App Store 與 Google Play 下
載榜首，印證臉書既有社群入口與用戶關係在 AI 產

品分發上的結構性優勢。Muse 雖為臉書打開訂閱、
交易與導購分潤、廣告業務強化三條潛在變現路徑，
但其成敗仍取決於 Muse 能否延續上線初期的動能、
擴展至美國以外市場，並深入用戶日常使用情境。
同時，Muse 的代理能力正引發平台間的摩擦。亞
馬遜隨即封鎖其代理購物功能，拒絕第三方代理人
直接在其平台上替用戶下單，銀行、保險、旅遊等
仰賴消費者惰性賺取溢價的產業也可能採取類似的
防禦措施，交易分潤路徑能否成立，將取決於臉書
與既有平台的合作或議價能力。
緊接在 Muse 之後，臉書近期也宣布成立企業 AI 平
台，並延攬 MongoDB 執行長 CJ Desai 領導此一新
事業，以圖將代理人技術從消費端延伸至企業端。
臉書在企業市場的最大資產是全球大量中小企業已
用來與客戶溝通的 WhatsApp。在 WhatsApp 基礎
上衍生的自動處理詢價、下單、售後客服的企業 AI
代理應用具有相當的想像空間。不過，本行認為臉
書企業平台執行的不確定性明顯高於 Muse。主要
仍是考量到中小企業的數據品質、客戶信任與資安

風險與消費端截然不同。臉書過去缺乏企業銷售團
隊與服務紀錄，從零建立企業級通路需要時間與額
外投入，或將加重臉書的資本與費用負擔。
因此，本行認為 Muse 僅回應了市場對臉書 AI 投資
變現的第一個問題「臉書有沒有 AI 產品」。第二個
問題「投入何時能回收」，仍有待後續進展驗證。

### Page 3

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
零售客流量表
• 谷歌整合式廣告業務營運未受
結構性衝擊，搜尋有自 AI 競
爭者收復失土的跡象。
• 但谷歌在 AI 模型層仍面臨 AI
推理商品化與旗艦模型空窗期
的壓力，不利市場信心。
3
《谷歌核心業務韌性無虞，但 AI 變現面臨商品化與
旗艦空窗雙重逆風》
9 月初聯邦法官駁回美國司法部拆分谷歌廣告技術
業務的訴求，改採行為矯正措施，意味谷歌廣告技
術堆疊得以保持完整，整合式廣告業務的營運模式
不受結構性衝擊，延續上半年 Android 應用商店案
和解落幕後法務風險持續收斂的趨勢。搜尋方面，
業界觀察顯示谷歌搜尋已在 ChatGPT 等 AI 競爭者
挑戰下收復部分使用量，AI Overviews 與 AI Mode

似乎有效留住用戶，緩解了 AI 聊天機器人侵蝕搜尋
入口的疑慮。
然而，谷歌在 AI 模型層面臨兩項壓力。第一是 AI
推理商品化。9 月下旬 OpenAI 推出 GPT-6 Sol 與
Luna 並將 API 價格砍半，Anthropic 亦發表 Opus
5.5，效能比肩前代頂級模型、成本卻降低 40%，並
同步上架三大雲端平台。前沿模型價格快速下探，
意味 Google Cloud 的 AI 推理營收單價與 Gemini
API 的定價空間在資本支出擴張之時同步受壓。價
格下行對使用量的影響動態將是市場在 10 月財報時
的觀察重點。
第二個壓力是旗艦模型的持續空窗。9 月下旬
DeepMind 新任主管暗示 Gemini 4 可望於年底前
推出，意味谷歌在 26Q4 之前將持續缺席前沿旗艦
競爭，但而同一期間 OpenAI 已推出 GPT-6、
Anthropic 推出 Opus 5.5。旗艦模型能力雖不必然
保證應用端的落地成效，但 Flash 等輕量模型與法
律、金融等垂直版本，多以旗艦模型為基礎蒸餾或

衍生，旗艦進度因此直接決定整體模型組合所能涵
蓋的應用場景廣度與深度。在上有閉源前沿模型、
下有低價開放權重模型持續滲透企業市場的夾擊下，
旗艦模型進度落後將持續壓抑市場對谷歌雲在企業
AI 應用的看法與信心。
